# Negative Prediction Rates by Checkpoint

This report enumerates every label at ranks 1, 2 and 3 for each cached checkpoint prediction file covering the complete 2,699-row V0 set. A row means: among responses where this checkpoint placed the listed label at the listed rank, how often was that label not the true label?

- Full-V0 checkpoint prediction caches: **63**.
- Rows in the rank/label table: **3,402** (18 labels × 3 ranks × 63 caches).
- Probabilities are ranked descending; exact ties preserve the saved canonical class-column order.
- `Wrong %` = `(support - correct) / support`. `Label prior` is the true-label frequency in the same V0 rows. `Below prior` marks candidate labels whose empirical precision at that rank is below their V0 base rate, a more meaningful negative signal than a high wrong percentage alone.
- The same V0 rows are reused by every cache. Counts across checkpoints or checkpoint versions are correlated and must not be summed as independent observations.
- These are descriptive V0 results. Checkpoint selection and some model development used V0; validate any rule using prompt-group cross-fitting before deployment.

## Summary at support ≥30

| Rank | Checkpoint-label pairs ≥90% wrong | Such pairs also below label prior | Model families represented |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 1 | 1 | 0 | 0 |
| 2 | 88 | 30 | 6 |
| 3 | 346 | 89 | 10 |

At rank 1, the single ≥90%-wrong pair is CatBoost predicting `gemini-3.6-flash` (103/113 wrong, 91.15%). Its precision is still above the 5.56% class prior, so it is not a negative-likelihood signal by the criterion above. Rank-2 and rank-3 entries should be interpreted conditionally by model, rank and label; lower ranks are naturally less accurate.
